claims:
- kind: summary
  text: Volume 3 Part IV of the Safety, Health and Environment (SHE) Manual establishes comprehensive environmental
    monitoring, dust control, air quality standards, and event contingency planning obligations for BMRCL Tender
    Package P1.
  evidence_ids:

    okf_sha256: 8a9850438ec160be318ccdedeb8465b51ba9850986edff681868f388391ea34a
    heading: Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT
    source_document: Vol3DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 1ee3fc8dfd12f659f34446b0a6db74e1e1cdd4d593ca91b2a48d59bf1ef97d52
    pdf_pages:
    - 73
    - 74

    - 76
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 7bea653d28db3a09695fc577706045a118cd6f049b1a965a72f0af31b2b73dbf
    json_pointer: /clauses/0
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"47.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 73,\n      \"text\": \"\
      47.0 ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT\\n\\n             47.1 ENVIRONMENTAL MONITORING\\n\\n              47.1.1 The\
      \  Contractor’s  Environmental  Team  shall  carry  out  the  monitoring  of\\n                     environmental\
      \ impacts during construction. Representative sensitive receivers in the\\n                           vicinity\
      \ of the works shall be monitored for noise and air quality impacts.\\n              47.1.2  For carrying\
      \ out impact monitoring for noise and air, equipment shall be provided,\\n                    operated and\
      \ maintained by the Contractor. The equipment shall be kept in a good\\n                       state  of \
      \ repair  in accordance  with  the  manufacturer’s recommendations and\\n                    maintained in\
      \ proper working order with sufficient spare equipment available in the\\n                     event of breakdown\
      \ to maintain the planned monitoring program.\\n              47.1.2 The calibration of monitoring instruments\
      \ and their respective calibrators shall be\\n                       carried out in accordance with the manufacturer’s\
      \ requirements to ensure they\\n                    perform to the same level of accuracy as stated in the\
      \ manufacturer’s specifications.\\n              47.1.3 Suspended Particulate Matter (SPM) levels shall be\
      \ measured by following the\\n                     standard high volume sampling method as set out in High\
      \ Volume Method for\\n                  Suspended Particulate, BIS: 5182-1981\\n              47.1.4  24-hour\
      \ average SPM concentration shall be measured by drawing air through a\\n                    High Volume Sampler\
      \ (HVS) fitted with pre-weighted Glass Fiber filter paper at an\\n                   average flow rate not\
      \ less than 1.1m3 per minute.\\n              47.1.5 The minimum requirements to the specifications of sound\
      \ level meter    should be\\n                   as given in IS: 9779-1981\\n              47.1.6  Engineer\
      \ will undertake baseline monitoring to establish background levels. Action\\n                      Level\
      \ of the Contractor shall be based on the results of baseline monitoring\\n                    program, which\
      \ will be made available to him prior to start of construction.\\n               47.1.7 The Contractor’s monitoring\
      \ program is summarized in Table –1.\\n\\n                                       TABLE-1\\n\\n           \
      \     SUMMARY OF CONTRACTOR’S MONITORING PROGRAMME\\n                Parameter                   Noise   \
      \                           Air\\n\\n          Sampling                Day Time (6.00 to 22.00 hrs):   RSPM,\
      \ SPM\\n                                             Lmax, Leq, L10, L90, L50            24-hours of the day\\\
      n                                        Night Time (9 PM – 6AM):      CO: 12 hrs from 8.00 to 20.00\\n  \
      \                                           Lmax, Leq, L10, L90, L50, Ldn          hrs.\\n\\n\\n         \
      \ Frequency               Once a week (when noise     Two 24-hour Samples every\\n           At each location\
      \              generating activities are           fifteen days at uniform intervals.\\n                 \
      \                     underway).\\n           Locations                To  be  determined  by  the  To be\
      \ determined by the\\n                                        Contractor  based  on  noise  Contractor based\
      \ on air sensitive\\n                                           sensitive receptors.              receptors.\\\
      n         Number of Locations        4 Locations                  2 Locations\\n           Duration  of  Monitoring\
      \ by  During Civil Construction        During Civil Construction\\n           Contractor\\n            Additional\
      \ Requirements     Adhoc monitoring as required.   Ad hoc monitoring as required\\n\\n\\n        47.2EVENT\
      \ CONTINGENCY PLAN\\n        The Contractor shall prepare an Event Contingency Plan under his Site Environmental\
      \ Plan. The\\n         purpose is to provide, in addition to monitoring activities, procedures for ensuring\
      \ that  if any\\n          environmental  exceedance  of  limiting  values  (either  accidental  or  through\
      \  inadequate\\n          implementation of mitigation measures on part of the Contractor) does occur, the\
      \ cause is\\n          quickly identified and remedied, and that the risk of a similar event recurring is\
      \ reduced.\\n\\n\\n          47.3      Air Quality\\n\\n\\n          47.3.1   The  Contractor  shall  take\
      \  all necessary  precautions  to minimise  fugitive  dust\\n                  emissions from operations involving\
      \ excavation, grading, and clearing of land and\\n                    disposal of waste. He shall not allow\
      \ emissions of fugitive dust from any transport,\\n                    handling, construction or storage activity\
      \ to remain visible in atmosphere beyond the\\n                    property line of emission source for any\
      \ prolonged period of time without notification to\\n                    the Employer.\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.2   The Contractor shall use construction equipment designed\
      \ and equipped to minimise\\n          or control air pollution. He shall maintain evidence of such design\
      \ and equipment and\\n       make these available for inspection by Employer.\\n\\n\\n47.3.3      If  after\
      \  commencement  of  construction  activity,  Employer  believes  that  the\\n          Contractor’s equipment\
      \ or methods of working are causing unacceptable air pollution\\n         impacts then these shall be inspected\
      \ and remedial proposals shall be drawn up by\\n          the Contractor, submitted for review to the Employer\
      \ and implemented.\\n\\n\\n47.3.4    In developing these remedial measures, the Contractor shall inspect and\
      \ review all\\n          dust sources that may be contributing to air pollution. Remedial measures include\
      \ use\\n           of additional / alternative equipment by the Contractor or maintenance / modification of\\\
      n           existing equipment of the Contractor.\\n\\n           In the event that approved remedial measures\
      \ are not being implemented and serious\\n         impacts persist, the Employer may direct the Contractor\
      \ to suspend work until the\\n        measures are implemented, as required under the Contract.\\n\\n\\n47.3.5\
      \    Contractor’s  transport  vehicles and  other equipment  shall conform  to emission\\n         standards\
      \  fixed by  Statutory  Agencies  of Government  of  India  or  the  State\\n        Government from time\
      \ to time. The Contractor shall carry out periodical checks and\\n         undertake remedial measures including\
      \ replacement,  if required, so as to operate\\n           within permissible norms.\\n\\n\\n47.3.6   The\
      \ Contractor shall establish and maintain records of routine maintenance program\\n            for internal\
      \ combustion engine powered vehicles and equipment used on this project.\\n       He shall keep records available\
      \ for inspection by Employer.\\n\\n\\n47.3.7   The Contractor shall cover loads of dust generating materials\
      \ like debris and soil being\\n          transported from construction  sites.  All trucks carrying loose\
      \ material should be\\n         covered and loaded with sufficient free- board to avoid spills through the\
      \ tailboard or\\n          sideboards.\\n\\n\\n47.3.8   The Contractor shall promptly transport all excavation\
      \ disposal materials of whatever\\n          kind so as not to delay work on the project. Stockpiling of materials\
      \  will only be\\n         allowed at sites designated by the Employer. The Contractor shall place excavation\\\
      n          materials in the dumping/disposal areas designated in the plans as given in the\\n           specifications.\\\
      n\\n\\n47.3.9   The temporary dumping areas shall be maintained by the Contractor at all times until\\n  \
      \        the excavate is re-utilised for backfilling or as directed by Employer. Dust control\\n         \
      \   activities shall continue even during any work stoppage.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n47.3.10  The Contractor shall place\
      \ material in a manner that will minimize dust production.\\n          Material shall be minimized each day\
      \ and wetted, to minimize dust production. During\\n          dry weather, dust control methods must be used\
      \ daily especially on windy, dry days to\\n         prevent any dust from blowing across the site perimeter.\\\
      n\\n\\n47.3.11  The Contractor shall water down construction sites as required to suppress dust,\\n      \
      \    during handling of excavation soil or debris or during demolition. The Contractor will\\n       make\
      \ water sprinklers, water supply and water delivering equipment available at any\\n         time that it is\
      \ required for dust control use. Dust screens will be used, as feasible when\\n           additional dust\
      \  control measures are needed  specially where the work  is near\\n          sensitive receptors.\\n\\n\\\
      n47.3.12  The Contractor shall provide a wash pit or a wheel washing and/or vehicle cleaning\\n          \
      \   facility at the exits from work sites such as construction depots and batching plants. At\\n         such\
      \ facility, high-pressure water jets will be directed at the wheels of vehicles to\\n        remove all spoil\
      \ and dirt.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.13  The Contractor shall design\
      \ and implement his blasting techniques so as to minimise\\n                     dust, noise, vibration generation\
      \ and prevention fly rock.\\n\\n\\n          47.3.14   Blasting technique should be consistent not only with\
      \ nature and quaintly of rock to be\\n                    blasted but also the location of blasting.\\n\\\
      n          47.3.15  The  contractor  shall  give  preference  to  explosives  with  better  environmental\\\
      n                      characteristics.\\n\\n          47.3.16  The Contractor shall protect structures, utilities,\
      \ pavements roads and other facilities\\n                  from disfiguration and damage as a result of his\
      \ activities. Where this is not possible,\\n                    the contractor  shall restore the structures,\
      \  utilities, pavements, roads and other\\n                          facilities to their original or better,\
      \ failing which the rectification/restoration work shall\\n                be carried out at the risk and\
      \ cost of the contractor.\\n\\n\\n          47.3.17  The Contractor shall submit to the Employer an Air Monitoring\
      \ and Control Plan\\n               (AMCP) under contract specific Site Environmental Plan to guide construction\
      \ activity\\n                     insofar as it relates to monitoring, controlling and mitigating air pollution.\\\
      n\\n\\n          47.4    AIR MONITORING\\n          47.4.1    Construction activities that will generate dust\
      \ impacts include excavation (including\\n                     related activities), material handling and\
      \ stockpiling, vehicular movement, and wind\\n                   erosion of unpaved work areas.\\n\\n    \
      \      47.4.2 The impact of fugitive dust on ambient air pollution depends on the quantity generated, as\\\
      n                     well as the drift potential of the dust particles injected into the atmosphere. Large\
      \ dust\\n                      particles will settle out near the source and smaller particles are likely\
      \ to undergo\\n                    dispersal over greater distance from the sources and impeded settling.\
      \ SPM levels will\\n                be monitored to evaluate the dust impact during the construction phase\
      \ of the Project.\\n\\n          47.4.3   The  Air  Quality  Monitoring and  Control Plan (AMCP)  in  contract-specific\
      \  Site\\n                   Environmental Plan prepared by the Contractor shall establish procedures to monitor\\\
      n                  impact air quality and measures to control air pollution including dust suppression due\\\
      n                      to construction activities at work sites. This plan shall contain description of activities\\\
      n                      that  will cause degradation  in  air  quality, environmental procedures  to manage\\\
      n                     pollutants to minimise the  air  pollution, monitoring program, record keeping and\\\
      n                      reporting.\\n\\n          47.4.4   The Engineer shall monitor Contractor’s performance\
      \ of tasks specified, and will inspect\\n                  necessary records, reports and procedures related\
      \ to the control of air quality given in\\n               AMCP.\\n          47.4.5    Information gathered\
      \ during the AMCP  will be catalogued and maintained by the\\n                    Contractor and shall be\
      \ available for review by the Engineer.\\n\\n          47.4.6   The exact location of the air monitoring stations\
      \ located near air sensitive receptors\\n                    adjoining the construction sites, such as residences,\
      \ schools, hotels and hospitals and\\n                  placement of monitoring equipment thereat shall be\
      \ agreed with the Engineer prior to\\n               commencement of air monitoring program.\\n\\n       \
      \   47.4.7   Impact monitoring during the course  of the Works  shall be carried out  at the\\n          \
      \          monitoring stations for two days (continuous twenty-four hours) every fifteen days and\\n     \
      \            where there is a perceived air quality problem.\\n          47.4.8   The Contractor shall construct\
      \ suitable fence, lockable gate, 220V AC power point and\\n                     suitable access at each air\
      \ monitoring station.  Monitoring stations shall be free from\\n                      local obstructions or\
      \ sheltering.\\n\\n\\n          47.4.9   Should impact monitoring record dust levels which are:\\n\\n\\n \
      \                      indicative of a deteriorating situation such that closer monitoring is reasonably\\\
      n                          indicated, or\\n\\n\\n                      when in the opinion of the Engineer\
      \ additional measurements are required in view\\n                          of deteriorating air quality,\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"text\": \"Then the Engineer may require the Contractor to\
      \ increase the frequency of impact\\n          monitoring at any one or more of the monitoring stations until\
      \ the results indicate an\\n         improving and acceptable level of air quality.\\n\\n\\n47.4.10  The Contractor\
      \ shall keep records of air quality monitoring (including location, date,\\n           time). The Contractor\
      \ shall submit a copy of monitoring results to the Engineer. The\\n           results should represent a \
      \ statistical evaluation of data by calculating maximum,\\n        minimum, mean, standard deviation, geometric\
      \ mean and percentile calculations for\\n          evaluation of frequency distribution, trends, and comparison\
      \ with emission standards.\\n\\n\\n47.4.11  The National Ambient Air Quality Standards given in Air (Prevention\
      \ and Control of\\n           Pollution) Act, 1981 may be referred by the Contractor for Limit Levels of SPM\
      \ in\\n         ambient  air which may be followed  in estimating the  pollution  level caused by\\n     \
      \     Contractor’s activities.\\n\\n47.4.12  Where  the  Engineer  determines  that  the  recorded  dust \
      \ (TSP)  level  is\\n          significantly  greater  than  the  Limit  levels,  the Engineer may  direct\
      \  the\\n         Contractor to take effective remedial measures including, but not limited to,\\n       \
      \  reviewing dust sources and modifying working procedures.\\n\\n47.4.13  Where the recorded baseline levels\
      \ exceed the ambient air quality standards, then at\\n         such locations the action level is the recorded\
      \ base line. Contractor shall take all\\n           effective remedial measures to contain the levels to their\
      \ baseline value as a result of\\n           his activities. The action level may be varied by and at the\
      \ sole discretion of the\\n         Engineer.\\n\\n47.4.14 The Contractor shall inform the Engineer of  all\
      \ steps taken to investigate cause of\\n        exceedance and immediate action taken to avoid further exceedance\
      \ through written\\n         reports and proposals for action under an Event Contingency Plan.\"\n    }\n\

      text\": \"          47.3.1   The  Contractor  shall  take  all necessary  precautions  to minimise  fugitive\

      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 73,\n      \"source_line_number\": 70,\n      \"text\": \"                    handling,\

      source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.2   The Contractor shall use construction equipment designed\
      \ and equipped to minimise\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n \

      : \"47.3.3      If  after  commencement  of  construction  activity,  Employer  believes  that  the\"\n  \

      : 11,\n      \"text\": \"          the Contractor, submitted for review to the Employer and implemented.\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n\

      \      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.4    In developing these\

      : 16,\n      \"text\": \"           of additional / alternative equipment by the Contractor or maintenance\

      \     \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"          47.3.13  The Contractor\
      \ shall design and implement his blasting techniques so as to minimise\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"                     dust, noise, vibration generation\
      \ and prevention fly rock.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"     \
      \     47.3.14   Blasting technique should be consistent not only with nature and quaintly of rock to be\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"            \
      \        blasted but also the location of blasting.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 10,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          47.3.15  The  contractor  shall  give  preference  to  explosives  with  better\

      : \"                      characteristics.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          47.3.16  The Contractor shall protect structures, utilities, pavements roads and\
      \ other facilities\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 15,\n      \"\
      text\": \"                  from disfiguration and damage as a result of his activities. Where this is not\

      \ \"                    the contractor  shall restore the structures,  utilities, pavements, roads and other\"\

      \                facilities to their original or better, failing which the rectification/restoration work\

      source_line_number\": 21,\n      \"text\": \"          47.3.17  The Contractor shall submit to the Employer\

      : 22,\n      \"text\": \"               (AMCP) under contract specific Site Environmental Plan to guide construction\
      \ activity\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"\

      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"          47.4.1\

      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 28,\n      \"text\": \"                     related\

      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 31,\n      \"text\": \"    \

      \           particles will settle out near the source and smaller particles are likely to undergo\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"                   \
      \ dispersal over greater distance from the sources and impeded settling. SPM levels will\"\n    },\n    {\n\

      : 38,\n      \"text\": \"                   Environmental Plan prepared by the Contractor shall establish\

      \"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"        Then\

      : 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"          monitoring at any one or more of the\

      \    \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"47.4.10  The Contractor shall keep records of air quality\
      \ monitoring (including location, date,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 9,\n      \"text\": \"           time). The Contractor shall submit a copy of monitoring results to the\
      \ Engineer. The\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 10,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"           results should represent a  statistical evaluation of data by calculating maximum,\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"        minimum, mean,\

      : 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"          evaluation of frequency distribution,\

      text\": \"47.4.11  The National Ambient Air Quality Standards given in Air (Prevention and Control of\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"           Pollution)\

      \    \"source_line_number\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 20,\n      \"text\": \"47.4.12  Where  the  Engineer  determines  that  the  recorded  dust  (TSP)  level\

  text: The contractor's environmental monitoring program requires noise monitoring at 4 locations once a week during
    active noise-generating operations and air quality monitoring (RSPM, SPM, CO) at 2 locations every fifteen days
    for two continuous 24-hour periods.

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"47.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 73,\n      \"text\": \"\
      47.0 ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT\\n\\n             47.1 ENVIRONMENTAL MONITORING\\n\\n              47.1.1 The\
      \  Contractor’s  Environmental  Team  shall  carry  out  the  monitoring  of\\n                     environmental\
      \ impacts during construction. Representative sensitive receivers in the\\n                           vicinity\
      \ of the works shall be monitored for noise and air quality impacts.\\n              47.1.2  For carrying\
      \ out impact monitoring for noise and air, equipment shall be provided,\\n                    operated and\
      \ maintained by the Contractor. The equipment shall be kept in a good\\n                       state  of \
      \ repair  in accordance  with  the  manufacturer’s recommendations and\\n                    maintained in\
      \ proper working order with sufficient spare equipment available in the\\n                     event of breakdown\
      \ to maintain the planned monitoring program.\\n              47.1.2 The calibration of monitoring instruments\
      \ and their respective calibrators shall be\\n                       carried out in accordance with the manufacturer’s\
      \ requirements to ensure they\\n                    perform to the same level of accuracy as stated in the\
      \ manufacturer’s specifications.\\n              47.1.3 Suspended Particulate Matter (SPM) levels shall be\
      \ measured by following the\\n                     standard high volume sampling method as set out in High\
      \ Volume Method for\\n                  Suspended Particulate, BIS: 5182-1981\\n              47.1.4  24-hour\
      \ average SPM concentration shall be measured by drawing air through a\\n                    High Volume Sampler\
      \ (HVS) fitted with pre-weighted Glass Fiber filter paper at an\\n                   average flow rate not\
      \ less than 1.1m3 per minute.\\n              47.1.5 The minimum requirements to the specifications of sound\
      \ level meter    should be\\n                   as given in IS: 9779-1981\\n              47.1.6  Engineer\
      \ will undertake baseline monitoring to establish background levels. Action\\n                      Level\
      \ of the Contractor shall be based on the results of baseline monitoring\\n                    program, which\
      \ will be made available to him prior to start of construction.\\n               47.1.7 The Contractor’s monitoring\
      \ program is summarized in Table –1.\\n\\n                                       TABLE-1\\n\\n           \
      \     SUMMARY OF CONTRACTOR’S MONITORING PROGRAMME\\n                Parameter                   Noise   \
      \                           Air\\n\\n          Sampling                Day Time (6.00 to 22.00 hrs):   RSPM,\
      \ SPM\\n                                             Lmax, Leq, L10, L90, L50            24-hours of the day\\\
      n                                        Night Time (9 PM – 6AM):      CO: 12 hrs from 8.00 to 20.00\\n  \
      \                                           Lmax, Leq, L10, L90, L50, Ldn          hrs.\\n\\n\\n         \
      \ Frequency               Once a week (when noise     Two 24-hour Samples every\\n           At each location\
      \              generating activities are           fifteen days at uniform intervals.\\n                 \
      \                     underway).\\n           Locations                To  be  determined  by  the  To be\
      \ determined by the\\n                                        Contractor  based  on  noise  Contractor based\
      \ on air sensitive\\n                                           sensitive receptors.              receptors.\\\
      n         Number of Locations        4 Locations                  2 Locations\\n           Duration  of  Monitoring\
      \ by  During Civil Construction        During Civil Construction\\n           Contractor\\n            Additional\
      \ Requirements     Adhoc monitoring as required.   Ad hoc monitoring as required\\n\\n\\n        47.2EVENT\
      \ CONTINGENCY PLAN\\n        The Contractor shall prepare an Event Contingency Plan under his Site Environmental\
      \ Plan. The\\n         purpose is to provide, in addition to monitoring activities, procedures for ensuring\
      \ that  if any\\n          environmental  exceedance  of  limiting  values  (either  accidental  or  through\
      \  inadequate\\n          implementation of mitigation measures on part of the Contractor) does occur, the\
      \ cause is\\n          quickly identified and remedied, and that the risk of a similar event recurring is\
      \ reduced.\\n\\n\\n          47.3      Air Quality\\n\\n\\n          47.3.1   The  Contractor  shall  take\
      \  all necessary  precautions  to minimise  fugitive  dust\\n                  emissions from operations involving\
      \ excavation, grading, and clearing of land and\\n                    disposal of waste. He shall not allow\
      \ emissions of fugitive dust from any transport,\\n                    handling, construction or storage activity\
      \ to remain visible in atmosphere beyond the\\n                    property line of emission source for any\
      \ prolonged period of time without notification to\\n                    the Employer.\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.2   The Contractor shall use construction equipment designed\
      \ and equipped to minimise\\n          or control air pollution. He shall maintain evidence of such design\
      \ and equipment and\\n       make these available for inspection by Employer.\\n\\n\\n47.3.3      If  after\
      \  commencement  of  construction  activity,  Employer  believes  that  the\\n          Contractor’s equipment\
      \ or methods of working are causing unacceptable air pollution\\n         impacts then these shall be inspected\
      \ and remedial proposals shall be drawn up by\\n          the Contractor, submitted for review to the Employer\
      \ and implemented.\\n\\n\\n47.3.4    In developing these remedial measures, the Contractor shall inspect and\
      \ review all\\n          dust sources that may be contributing to air pollution. Remedial measures include\
      \ use\\n           of additional / alternative equipment by the Contractor or maintenance / modification of\\\
      n           existing equipment of the Contractor.\\n\\n           In the event that approved remedial measures\
      \ are not being implemented and serious\\n         impacts persist, the Employer may direct the Contractor\
      \ to suspend work until the\\n        measures are implemented, as required under the Contract.\\n\\n\\n47.3.5\
      \    Contractor’s  transport  vehicles and  other equipment  shall conform  to emission\\n         standards\
      \  fixed by  Statutory  Agencies  of Government  of  India  or  the  State\\n        Government from time\
      \ to time. The Contractor shall carry out periodical checks and\\n         undertake remedial measures including\
      \ replacement,  if required, so as to operate\\n           within permissible norms.\\n\\n\\n47.3.6   The\
      \ Contractor shall establish and maintain records of routine maintenance program\\n            for internal\
      \ combustion engine powered vehicles and equipment used on this project.\\n       He shall keep records available\
      \ for inspection by Employer.\\n\\n\\n47.3.7   The Contractor shall cover loads of dust generating materials\
      \ like debris and soil being\\n          transported from construction  sites.  All trucks carrying loose\
      \ material should be\\n         covered and loaded with sufficient free- board to avoid spills through the\
      \ tailboard or\\n          sideboards.\\n\\n\\n47.3.8   The Contractor shall promptly transport all excavation\
      \ disposal materials of whatever\\n          kind so as not to delay work on the project. Stockpiling of materials\
      \  will only be\\n         allowed at sites designated by the Employer. The Contractor shall place excavation\\\
      n          materials in the dumping/disposal areas designated in the plans as given in the\\n           specifications.\\\
      n\\n\\n47.3.9   The temporary dumping areas shall be maintained by the Contractor at all times until\\n  \
      \        the excavate is re-utilised for backfilling or as directed by Employer. Dust control\\n         \
      \   activities shall continue even during any work stoppage.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n47.3.10  The Contractor shall place\
      \ material in a manner that will minimize dust production.\\n          Material shall be minimized each day\
      \ and wetted, to minimize dust production. During\\n          dry weather, dust control methods must be used\
      \ daily especially on windy, dry days to\\n         prevent any dust from blowing across the site perimeter.\\\
      n\\n\\n47.3.11  The Contractor shall water down construction sites as required to suppress dust,\\n      \
      \    during handling of excavation soil or debris or during demolition. The Contractor will\\n       make\
      \ water sprinklers, water supply and water delivering equipment available at any\\n         time that it is\
      \ required for dust control use. Dust screens will be used, as feasible when\\n           additional dust\
      \  control measures are needed  specially where the work  is near\\n          sensitive receptors.\\n\\n\\\
      n47.3.12  The Contractor shall provide a wash pit or a wheel washing and/or vehicle cleaning\\n          \
      \   facility at the exits from work sites such as construction depots and batching plants. At\\n         such\
      \ facility, high-pressure water jets will be directed at the wheels of vehicles to\\n        remove all spoil\
      \ and dirt.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.13  The Contractor shall design\
      \ and implement his blasting techniques so as to minimise\\n                     dust, noise, vibration generation\
      \ and prevention fly rock.\\n\\n\\n          47.3.14   Blasting technique should be consistent not only with\
      \ nature and quaintly of rock to be\\n                    blasted but also the location of blasting.\\n\\\
      n          47.3.15  The  contractor  shall  give  preference  to  explosives  with  better  environmental\\\
      n                      characteristics.\\n\\n          47.3.16  The Contractor shall protect structures, utilities,\
      \ pavements roads and other facilities\\n                  from disfiguration and damage as a result of his\
      \ activities. Where this is not possible,\\n                    the contractor  shall restore the structures,\
      \  utilities, pavements, roads and other\\n                          facilities to their original or better,\
      \ failing which the rectification/restoration work shall\\n                be carried out at the risk and\
      \ cost of the contractor.\\n\\n\\n          47.3.17  The Contractor shall submit to the Employer an Air Monitoring\
      \ and Control Plan\\n               (AMCP) under contract specific Site Environmental Plan to guide construction\
      \ activity\\n                     insofar as it relates to monitoring, controlling and mitigating air pollution.\\\
      n\\n\\n          47.4    AIR MONITORING\\n          47.4.1    Construction activities that will generate dust\
      \ impacts include excavation (including\\n                     related activities), material handling and\
      \ stockpiling, vehicular movement, and wind\\n                   erosion of unpaved work areas.\\n\\n    \
      \      47.4.2 The impact of fugitive dust on ambient air pollution depends on the quantity generated, as\\\
      n                     well as the drift potential of the dust particles injected into the atmosphere. Large\
      \ dust\\n                      particles will settle out near the source and smaller particles are likely\
      \ to undergo\\n                    dispersal over greater distance from the sources and impeded settling.\
      \ SPM levels will\\n                be monitored to evaluate the dust impact during the construction phase\
      \ of the Project.\\n\\n          47.4.3   The  Air  Quality  Monitoring and  Control Plan (AMCP)  in  contract-specific\
      \  Site\\n                   Environmental Plan prepared by the Contractor shall establish procedures to monitor\\\
      n                  impact air quality and measures to control air pollution including dust suppression due\\\
      n                      to construction activities at work sites. This plan shall contain description of activities\\\
      n                      that  will cause degradation  in  air  quality, environmental procedures  to manage\\\
      n                     pollutants to minimise the  air  pollution, monitoring program, record keeping and\\\
      n                      reporting.\\n\\n          47.4.4   The Engineer shall monitor Contractor’s performance\
      \ of tasks specified, and will inspect\\n                  necessary records, reports and procedures related\
      \ to the control of air quality given in\\n               AMCP.\\n          47.4.5    Information gathered\
      \ during the AMCP  will be catalogued and maintained by the\\n                    Contractor and shall be\
      \ available for review by the Engineer.\\n\\n          47.4.6   The exact location of the air monitoring stations\
      \ located near air sensitive receptors\\n                    adjoining the construction sites, such as residences,\
      \ schools, hotels and hospitals and\\n                  placement of monitoring equipment thereat shall be\
      \ agreed with the Engineer prior to\\n               commencement of air monitoring program.\\n\\n       \
      \   47.4.7   Impact monitoring during the course  of the Works  shall be carried out  at the\\n          \
      \          monitoring stations for two days (continuous twenty-four hours) every fifteen days and\\n     \
      \            where there is a perceived air quality problem.\\n          47.4.8   The Contractor shall construct\
      \ suitable fence, lockable gate, 220V AC power point and\\n                     suitable access at each air\
      \ monitoring station.  Monitoring stations shall be free from\\n                      local obstructions or\
      \ sheltering.\\n\\n\\n          47.4.9   Should impact monitoring record dust levels which are:\\n\\n\\n \
      \                      indicative of a deteriorating situation such that closer monitoring is reasonably\\\
      n                          indicated, or\\n\\n\\n                      when in the opinion of the Engineer\
      \ additional measurements are required in view\\n                          of deteriorating air quality,\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"text\": \"Then the Engineer may require the Contractor to\
      \ increase the frequency of impact\\n          monitoring at any one or more of the monitoring stations until\
      \ the results indicate an\\n         improving and acceptable level of air quality.\\n\\n\\n47.4.10  The Contractor\
      \ shall keep records of air quality monitoring (including location, date,\\n           time). The Contractor\
      \ shall submit a copy of monitoring results to the Engineer. The\\n           results should represent a \
      \ statistical evaluation of data by calculating maximum,\\n        minimum, mean, standard deviation, geometric\
      \ mean and percentile calculations for\\n          evaluation of frequency distribution, trends, and comparison\
      \ with emission standards.\\n\\n\\n47.4.11  The National Ambient Air Quality Standards given in Air (Prevention\
      \ and Control of\\n           Pollution) Act, 1981 may be referred by the Contractor for Limit Levels of SPM\
      \ in\\n         ambient  air which may be followed  in estimating the  pollution  level caused by\\n     \
      \     Contractor’s activities.\\n\\n47.4.12  Where  the  Engineer  determines  that  the  recorded  dust \
      \ (TSP)  level  is\\n          significantly  greater  than  the  Limit  levels,  the Engineer may  direct\
      \  the\\n         Contractor to take effective remedial measures including, but not limited to,\\n       \
      \  reviewing dust sources and modifying working procedures.\\n\\n47.4.13  Where the recorded baseline levels\
      \ exceed the ambient air quality standards, then at\\n         such locations the action level is the recorded\
      \ base line. Contractor shall take all\\n           effective remedial measures to contain the levels to their\
      \ baseline value as a result of\\n           his activities. The action level may be varied by and at the\
      \ sole discretion of the\\n         Engineer.\\n\\n47.4.14 The Contractor shall inform the Engineer of  all\
      \ steps taken to investigate cause of\\n        exceedance and immediate action taken to avoid further exceedance\
      \ through written\\n         reports and proposals for action under an Event Contingency Plan.\"\n    }\n\

      text\": \"          47.3.1   The  Contractor  shall  take  all necessary  precautions  to minimise  fugitive\
      \  dust\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 73,\n      \"source_line_number\": 68,\n      \"text\": \" \
      \                 emissions from operations involving excavation, grading, and clearing of land and\"\n  \

      text\": \"47.3.6   The Contractor shall establish and maintain records of routine maintenance program\"\n\

      text\": \"47.3.7   The Contractor shall cover loads of dust generating materials like debris and soil being\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"          transported\

      : 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 38,\n      \"text\": \"         covered and loaded with sufficient free-\
      \ board to avoid spills through the tailboard or\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 39,\n      \"text\": \"          sideboards.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 40,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 41,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 42,\n      \"\
      text\": \"47.3.8   The Contractor shall promptly transport all excavation disposal materials of whatever\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 43,\n      \"text\": \"          kind\
      \ so as not to delay work on the project. Stockpiling of materials  will only be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 44,\n      \"text\": \"         allowed at sites designated\
      \ by the Employer. The Contractor shall place excavation\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n     \
      \ \"source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\": \"          materials in the dumping/disposal areas designated\
      \ in the plans as given in the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"           specifications.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 47,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 48,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"\
      text\": \"47.3.9   The temporary dumping areas shall be maintained by the Contractor at all times until\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 50,\n      \"text\": \"          the\
      \ excavate is re-utilised for backfilling or as directed by Employer. Dust control\"\n    },\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 51,\n      \"text\": \"            activities shall continue\
      \ even during any work stoppage.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\":\
      \ 52,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n \

      text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 55,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 56,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.10 \
      \ The Contractor shall place material in a manner that will minimize dust production.\"\n    },\n    {\n \
      \     \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 57,\n      \"text\": \"          Material shall be\
      \ minimized each day and wetted, to minimize dust production. During\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 58,\n      \"text\": \"          dry weather, dust control methods must\
      \ be used daily especially on windy, dry days to\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 59,\n      \"text\": \"         prevent any dust from blowing across the site perimeter.\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 60,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n   \
      \   \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 61,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 62,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.11  The Contractor shall water down construction\
      \ sites as required to suppress dust,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 63,\n      \"text\": \"          during handling of excavation soil or debris or during demolition. The\
      \ Contractor will\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 64,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"       make water sprinklers, water supply and water delivering equipment available at any\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 65,\n      \"text\": \"         time that it\
      \ is required for dust control use. Dust screens will be used, as feasible when\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 66,\n      \"text\": \"           additional dust  control\
      \ measures are needed  specially where the work  is near\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n     \
      \ \"source_line_number\": 67,\n      \"text\": \"          sensitive receptors.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 68,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 69,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 70,\n      \"text\": \"47.3.12  The Contractor shall provide a wash pit or a\
      \ wheel washing and/or vehicle cleaning\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 71,\n      \"text\": \"             facility at the exits from work sites such as construction depots and\
      \ batching plants. At\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 72,\n     \
      \ \"text\": \"         such facility, high-pressure water jets will be directed at the wheels of vehicles\
      \ to\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 73,\n      \"text\": \"    \
      \    remove all spoil and dirt.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 74,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"source_line_number\": 75,\n      \"\

      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n \
      \     \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"          47.3.13  The Contractor\
      \ shall design and implement his blasting techniques so as to minimise\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"                     dust, noise, vibration generation\
      \ and prevention fly rock.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"     \
      \     47.3.14   Blasting technique should be consistent not only with nature and quaintly of rock to be\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"            \
      \        blasted but also the location of blasting.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 10,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          47.3.15  The  contractor  shall  give  preference  to  explosives  with  better\
      \  environmental\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                      characteristics.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          47.3.16  The Contractor shall protect structures, utilities, pavements roads and\
      \ other facilities\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 15,\n      \"\
      text\": \"                  from disfiguration and damage as a result of his activities. Where this is not\
      \ possible,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\":\
      \ \"                    the contractor  shall restore the structures,  utilities, pavements, roads and other\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \                facilities to their original or better, failing which the rectification/restoration work\
      \ shall\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \" \
      \               be carried out at the risk and cost of the contractor.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 20,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 21,\n      \"text\": \"          47.3.17  The Contractor shall submit to the Employer\
      \ an Air Monitoring and Control Plan\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 22,\n      \"text\": \"               (AMCP) under contract specific Site Environmental Plan to guide construction\
      \ activity\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \                     insofar as it relates to monitoring, controlling and mitigating air pollution.\"\n \
      \   },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 25,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n      \"text\": \"          47.4    AIR MONITORING\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"          47.4.1\
      \    Construction activities that will generate dust impacts include excavation (including\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 28,\n      \"text\": \"                     related\
      \ activities), material handling and stockpiling, vehicular movement, and wind\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 29,\n      \"text\": \"                   erosion of unpaved\
      \ work areas.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 30,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 31,\n      \"text\": \"    \
      \      47.4.2 The impact of fugitive dust on ambient air pollution depends on the quantity generated, as\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \           well as the drift potential of the dust particles injected into the atmosphere. Large dust\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"           \
      \           particles will settle out near the source and smaller particles are likely to undergo\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"                   \
      \ dispersal over greater distance from the sources and impeded settling. SPM levels will\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"                be monitored\
      \ to evaluate the dust impact during the construction phase of the Project.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"          47.4.3   The  Air  Quality  Monitoring and\
      \  Control Plan (AMCP)  in  contract-specific  Site\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 38,\n      \"text\": \"                   Environmental Plan prepared by the Contractor shall establish\
      \ procedures to monitor\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 39,\n   \
      \   \"text\": \"                  impact air quality and measures to control air pollution including dust\
      \ suppression due\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 40,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                      to construction activities at work sites. This plan shall contain description of\
      \ activities\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 41,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                      that  will cause degradation  in  air  quality, environmental procedures  to manage\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 42,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \           pollutants to minimise the  air  pollution, monitoring program, record keeping and\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 43,\n      \"text\": \"                   \
      \   reporting.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 44,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\": \"    \
      \      47.4.4   The Engineer shall monitor Contractor’s performance of tasks specified, and will inspect\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \        necessary records, reports and procedures related to the control of air quality given in\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 47,\n      \"text\": \"               AMCP.\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 48,\n      \"text\": \"          47.4.5\
      \    Information gathered during the AMCP  will be catalogued and maintained by the\"\n    },\n    {\n   \
      \   \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"                    Contractor\
      \ and shall be available for review by the Engineer.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 50,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 51,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          47.4.6   The exact location of the air monitoring stations located near air sensitive\
      \ receptors\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 52,\n      \"text\":\
      \ \"                    adjoining the construction sites, such as residences, schools, hotels and hospitals\
      \ and\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n      \"text\": \"   \
      \               placement of monitoring equipment thereat shall be agreed with the Engineer prior to\"\n \
      \   },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"            \
      \   commencement of air monitoring program.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 55,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 56,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          47.4.7   Impact monitoring during the course  of the Works  shall be carried out\
      \  at the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 57,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \                    monitoring stations for two days (continuous twenty-four hours) every fifteen days and\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 58,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \       where there is a perceived air quality problem.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 59,\n      \"text\": \"          47.4.8   The Contractor shall construct suitable fence,\
      \ lockable gate, 220V AC power point and\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 60,\n      \"text\": \"                     suitable access at each air monitoring station.  Monitoring\
      \ stations shall be free from\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 61,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"                      local obstructions or sheltering.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 62,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 63,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 64,\n      \"text\": \"          47.4.9   Should impact monitoring record dust levels\
      \ which are:\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 65,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 66,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 67,\n      \"text\": \"           \
      \            indicative of a deteriorating situation such that closer monitoring is reasonably\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 68,\n      \"text\": \"                   \
      \       indicated, or\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 69,\n     \
      \ \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 70,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 71,\n      \"text\": \"    \
      \                  when in the opinion of the Engineer additional measurements are required in view\"\n \
      \   },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 72,\n      \"text\": \"            \
      \              of deteriorating air quality,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 73,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 74,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 75,\n      \"\
      text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"source_line_number\": 76,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"        Then\
      \ the Engineer may require the Contractor to increase the frequency of impact\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"          monitoring at any one or more of the\
      \ monitoring stations until the results indicate an\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 5,\n      \"text\": \"         improving and acceptable level of air quality.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n  \
      \    \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"47.4.10  The Contractor shall keep records of air quality\
      \ monitoring (including location, date,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 9,\n      \"text\": \"           time). The Contractor shall submit a copy of monitoring results to the\
      \ Engineer. The\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 10,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"           results should represent a  statistical evaluation of data by calculating maximum,\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"        minimum, mean,\
      \ standard deviation, geometric mean and percentile calculations for\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"          evaluation of frequency distribution,\
      \ trends, and comparison with emission standards.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 15,\n      \"\
      text\": \"47.4.11  The National Ambient Air Quality Standards given in Air (Prevention and Control of\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"           Pollution)\
      \ Act, 1981 may be referred by the Contractor for Limit Levels of SPM in\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"         ambient  air which may be followed  in\
      \ estimating the  pollution  level caused by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 18,\n      \"text\": \"          Contractor’s activities.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n  \
      \    \"source_line_number\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 20,\n      \"text\": \"47.4.12  Where  the  Engineer  determines  that  the  recorded  dust  (TSP)  level\
      \  is\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 21,\n      \"text\": \"   \
      \       significantly  greater  than  the  Limit  levels,  the Engineer may  direct  the\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n      \"text\": \"         Contractor to take\
      \ effective remedial measures including, but not limited to,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"         reviewing dust sources and modifying working\
      \ procedures.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 25,\n      \"text\": \"47.4.13\
      \  Where the recorded baseline levels exceed the ambient air quality standards, then at\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n      \"text\": \"         such locations the\
      \ action level is the recorded base line. Contractor shall take all\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\":\
      \ 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"           effective remedial measures to contain\
      \ the levels to their baseline value as a result of\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 28,\n      \"text\": \"           his activities. The action level may be varied by and at the sole discretion\
      \ of the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 29,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \         Engineer.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 30,\n      \"\
      text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 31,\n      \"text\": \"\
      47.4.14 The Contractor shall inform the Engineer of  all steps taken to investigate cause of\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"        exceedance and\
      \ immediate action taken to avoid further exceedance through written\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"         reports and proposals for action under\
      \ an Event Contingency Plan.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 34,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"source_line_number\": 35,\n      \"\
