    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 78,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n  \
      \                                                        262\\n\\n50.4    A suggested list of trees  / shrubs\
      \ suitable for planting and landscaping is found in\\n         Employer’s Project SHE Manual.\\n\\n\\n51.0\
      \      Felling of Trees\\n\\n\\n51.1    The contractor shall identify the number and type of trees that are\
      \ require to be felled\\n         as a result of construction of works and facilities related to Bangalore\
      \ Metro Project\\n        and inform the Employer.\\n\\n\\n51.2       All trees and shrubbery, which are not\
      \ specifically require to be cleared or removed for\\n          construction purposes, shall be preserved\
      \ and shall be protected from any damage\\n           that may be caused by Contractor’s construction operations\
      \ and equipment. The\\n          contractor shall not fell, remove or dispose of any tree or forest produce\
      \ in any land\\n        handed over to him for the construction of works and facilities related to Bangalore\\\
      n         Metro except with the previous permission obtained from the Forest Department.\\n\\n\\n51.3    The\
      \ Employer shall arrange permission from the forest department for trees to be\\n           felled or transplanted.\
      \ The Employer will permit the removal of trees or shrubs only\\n           after prior approval.\\n\\n51.4\
      \     Special care shall be exercised where trees or shrubs are exposed to injuries by\\n          construction\
      \ equipment, blasting, excavating, dumping, chemical damage or other\\n          operation and the Contractor\
      \ shall adequately protect such trees by used of protective\\n           barriers or other methods approved\
      \ by the Employer. Trees shall not be used for\\n         anchorage.\\n\\n\\n52.0     Fly Ash\\n\\n\\n52.1\
      \    The Employer may require the contractor to use fly ash as a percentage substitution of\\n         cement,\
      \ in concrete for certain structures and works.\\n\\n\\n52.2      In all such uses of Fly Ash, the contractor\
      \ shall maintain a detailed record of usage of\\n          Fly Ash. The contractor shall also collect related\
      \ details and provide to the Employer.\\n\\n\\n52.3    The reporting details on consumption of Fly Ash are\
      \ found in Employer’s SHE Manual.\\n\\n\\n53.0    Waste\\n\\n\\n53.1    The contractor is required to develop,\
      \ institute and maintain a Waste Management\\n        Programme (WMP) during the construction of the project\
      \ for his works, which may\\n          include: -\\n\\n\\n                i)     Identification of disposal\
      \ sites.\\n\\n                  ii)     Identification of quantities to be excavated and disposed off.\\n\\\
      n                  iii)    Identification of split between waste and inert material\\n\\n             iv)\
      \    Identification of amounts intended to be stored temporarily on site location of\\n              such\
      \ storage.\\n\\n           v)     Identification of intended transport means and route.\\n\\n            \
      \ vi)   Obtaining permission, where required, for disposal.\\n\\n\\n53.2     Such a mechanism  is intended\
      \ to ensure that the designation of areas  for the\\n         segregation and  temporary  storage  of  reusable\
      \ and  recyclable  materials  are\\n          incorporate into the WMP. The WMP should be prepared and submitted\
      \ to the\\n         Engineer for approval.\\n\\n53.3    The Contractor shall handle waste in a manner that\
      \ ensures they are held securely\\n          without loss or leakage thus minimizing potential for pollution.\
      \ The Contractor shall\\n          maintain and clean waste storage areas regularly.\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 74 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        47.039981842041016,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        561.0993041992188,\n\
      \        24.210372924804688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \"                    \
      \                                                                                                        \
      \       Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n\
      \        26.57955551147461,\n        85.20714569091797,\n        35.93199157714844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n        809.042724609375,\n\
      \        508.6270751953125,\n        830.5714111328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                              September 2026         Section Page\
      \ 74 of 114 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.43965911865234,\n        38.09945297241211,\n\
      \        512.76806640625,\n        587.9320678710938\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n50.4  \\nA suggested list of trees / shrubs suitable for planting and landscaping is found in \\nEmployer’s\
      \ Project SHE Manual. \\n \\n51.0 \\nFelling of Trees \\n \\n51.1  \\nThe contractor shall identify the number\
      \ and type of trees that are require to be felled \\nas a result of construction of works and facilities related\
      \ to Bangalore Metro Project \\nand inform the Employer. \\n \\n51.2 \\nAll trees and shrubbery, which are\
      \ not specifically require to be cleared or removed for \\nconstruction purposes, shall be preserved and shall\
      \ be protected from any damage \\nthat may be caused by Contractor’s construction operations and equipment.\
      \ The \\ncontractor shall not fell, remove or dispose of any tree or forest produce in any land \\nhanded\
      \ over to him for the construction of works and facilities related to Bangalore \\nMetro except with the previous\
      \ permission obtained from the Forest Department.  \\n \\n51.3 \\nThe Employer shall arrange permission from\
      \ the forest department for trees to be \\nfelled or transplanted. The Employer will permit the removal of\
      \ trees or shrubs only \\nafter prior approval. \\n51.4 \\nSpecial care shall be exercised where trees or\
      \ shrubs are exposed to injuries by \\nconstruction equipment, blasting, excavating, dumping, chemical damage\
      \ or other \\noperation and the Contractor shall adequately protect such trees by used of protective \\nbarriers\
      \ or other methods approved by the Employer. Trees shall not be used for \\nanchorage.  \\n \\n52.0 \\nFly\
      \ Ash \\n \\n52.1 \\nThe Employer may require the contractor to use fly ash as a percentage substitution of\
      \ \\ncement, in concrete for certain structures and works. \\n \\n52.2  \\nIn all such uses of Fly Ash, the\
      \ contractor shall maintain a detailed record of usage of \\nFly Ash. The contractor shall also collect related\
      \ details and provide to the Employer. \\n \\n52.3 \\nThe reporting details on consumption of Fly Ash are\
      \ found in Employer’s SHE Manual. \\n \\n53.0 \\nWaste \\n \\n53.1 \\nThe contractor is required to develop,\
      \ institute and maintain a Waste Management \\nProgramme (WMP) during the construction of the project for\
      \ his works, which may \\ninclude: - \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.44164276123047,\n\
      \        593.0995483398438,\n        512.77001953125,\n        787.4920043945312\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 4,\n      \"text\": \"i) \\nIdentification of disposal sites. \\nii) \\nIdentification of quantities to\
      \ be excavated and disposed off. \\niii) \\nIdentification of split between waste and inert material \\niv)\
      \ \\nIdentification of amounts intended to be stored temporarily on site location of \\nsuch storage. \\nv)\
      \ \\nIdentification of intended transport means and route. \\nvi) \\nObtaining permission, where required,\
      \ for disposal. \\n \\n53.2  \\nSuch a mechanism is intended to ensure that the designation of areas for the\
      \ \\nsegregation and temporary storage of reusable and recyclable materials are \\nincorporate into the WMP.\
      \ The WMP should be prepared and submitted to the \\nEngineer for approval.  \\n53.3  \\nThe Contractor shall\
      \ handle waste in a manner that ensures they are held securely \\nwithout loss or leakage thus minimizing\
      \ potential for pollution.  The Contractor shall \\nmaintain and clean waste storage areas regularly. \\n\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n\
      \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"262\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n\
      }"

    heading: 'Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management'
    source_document: Vol3DLNP1.pdf

    json_path: parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 79,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n263\\\
      n          53.4    The Contractor shall remove waste in a timely manner and disposed off at landfill sites\\\
      n                       after obtaining approval of the competent authorities namely BBMP, BDA, BMRDA,\\n\
      \              BWSSB.\\n\\n\\n          53.5     Burning of wastes is prohibited. The Contractor shall not\
      \ burn debris or vegetation or\\n                    construction waste on the site but remove it in accordance\
      \ with 50.1 above.\\n\\n\\n          53.6    The Contractor shall make arrangement to dispose of metal scrap\
      \ and other saleable\\n                 waste to authorized dealer and make available to the Employer on request,\
      \ records of\\n                 such sales.\\n\\n          54.0     Hazardous Waste Management\\n\\n     \
      \     54.1         If encountered or generated as a result of Contractor’s activity, then waste classified\\\
      n                 as hazardous under the “Hazardous Wastes (Management & Handling) Rules, 1989,\\n       \
      \         amendments 2000, 2003” shall be disposed off in a manner in compliance with the\\n             \
      \     procedure given in the rules under the aforesaid act.\\n\\n\\n          54.2     Chemicals classified\
      \ as hazardous chemicals under “Manufacture, Storage and Import\\n                      of Hazardous Chemical\
      \ Rules, 1989 of Environment (Protection) Act, 1986 shall be\\n                  disposed off in a manner\
      \ in compliance with the procedure given in the rules under the\\n                    aforesaid act.\\n\\\
      n\\n          54.3    The contractor shall identify the nature and quantity of hazardous waste generated as\\\
      n                a result of his activities and shall file a ‘Request for Authorisation’ with Bangalore\\\
      n                     Pollution Control Board along with a map showing the location of storage area.\\n\\\
      n\\n          54.4     Outside the storage area, the contractor shall place a ‘display board’, which  will\\\
      n                    display quantity and nature of hazardous waste, on date. Hazardous Waste needs to\\n\
      \                be stored in a secure place\\n\\n\\n          54.5         It shall be the responsibility\
      \ of the contractor to ensure that hazardous wastes are\\n                     stored, based on the composition,\
      \ in a manner suitable for handling, storage and\\n                      transport. The labelling and packaging\
      \ is required to be easily visible and be able to\\n                   withstand physical conditions and climatic\
      \ factors.\\n\\n\\n          54.6    The contractor shall approach only Authorised Recyclers of Hazardous\
      \ Waste for\\n                    disposal of Hazardous Waste, under intimation to the Employer.\\n\\n\\n\
      \          54.7      Submittal of all environment related documents and records pertaining to monitoring\\\
      n                and trend analysis on key parameters such as but not limited to consumption/efficient\\n\
      \                 use of resources such as energy, water, material such as cement, fly ash, iron and\\n  \
      \                     steel, recycle/reuse of waste etc that shall have demonstrated continual improvement\\\
      n                        in the implementation of Environmental management System. Failure to do so the\\\
      n                  employer shall impose appropriate penalty as indicated under penalty clause.\\n\\n    \
      \      55.0     Energy Management\\n\\n          55.1    The contractor shall use and maintain equipment so\
      \ as to conserve energy and shall\\n                be able to produce demonstrable evidence of the same upon\
      \ Employer’s request.\\n\\n\\n          55.2     Measures to conserve energy include but not limited to the\
      \ following:\\n\\n                                 i)    Use of energy efficient motors and pumps\\n\\n  \
      \                                  ii)   Use of energy efficient lighting, which uses energy efficient luminaries\\\
      n\\n                                     iii)   Adequate and uniform illumination level at construction sites\
      \ suitable for the task\\n\\n                          iv)   Proper size and length of cables and wires to\
      \ match the rating of equipment\\n\\n                      v)   Use of energy efficient air conditioners\\\
      n\\n\\n          55.3   The contractor shall design site offices maximum daylight and minimum heat gain. The\\\
      n               rooms shall be well insulated to enhance the efficiency of air conditioners and the use of\\\
      n                   solar films on windows may be used where feasible.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n         Bangalore Metro\
      \ Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 75 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        50.15998077392578,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        564.21923828125,\n\
      \        24.210372924804688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \"                    \
      \                                                                                                        \
      \       Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n\
      \        26.57955551147461,\n        88.32714080810547,\n        35.93199157714844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n        809.042724609375,\n\
      \        511.7470703125,\n        830.5714111328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                              September 2026         Section Page\
      \ 75 of 114 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.55926513671875,\n        38.09945297241211,\n\
      \        518.7679443359375,\n        778.4917602539062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"53.4  \\nThe Contractor shall remove waste in a timely manner and disposed off at landfill sites \\nafter\
      \ obtaining approval of the competent authorities namely BBMP, BDA, BMRDA, \\nBWSSB. \\n \\n53.5  \\nBurning\
      \ of wastes is prohibited. The Contractor shall not burn debris or vegetation or \\nconstruction waste on\
      \ the site but remove it in accordance with 50.1 above.  \\n \\n53.6  \\nThe Contractor shall make arrangement\
      \ to dispose of metal scrap and other saleable \\nwaste to authorized dealer and make available to the Employer\
      \ on request, records of \\nsuch sales.    \\n54.0 \\nHazardous Waste Management \\n54.1  \\nIf encountered\
      \ or generated as a result of Contractor’s activity, then waste classified \\nas hazardous under the “Hazardous\
      \ Wastes (Management & Handling) Rules, 1989, \\namendments 2000, 2003” shall be disposed off in a manner\
      \ in compliance with the \\nprocedure given in the rules under the aforesaid act.   \\n \\n54.2 \\nChemicals\
      \ classified as hazardous chemicals under “Manufacture, Storage and Import \\nof Hazardous Chemical Rules,\
      \ 1989 of Environment (Protection) Act, 1986 shall be \\ndisposed off in a manner in compliance with the procedure\
      \ given in the rules under the \\naforesaid act.   \\n \\n54.3 \\nThe contractor shall identify the nature\
      \ and quantity of hazardous waste generated as \\na result of his activities and shall file a ‘Request for\
      \ Authorisation’ with Bangalore  \\nPollution Control Board along with a map showing the location of storage\
      \ area.  \\n \\n54.4 \\nOutside the storage area, the contractor shall place a ‘display board’, which will\
      \ \\ndisplay quantity and nature of hazardous waste, on date. Hazardous Waste needs to \\nbe stored in a secure\
      \ place \\n \\n54.5 \\nIt shall be the responsibility of the contractor to ensure that hazardous wastes are\
      \ \\nstored, based on the composition, in a manner suitable for handling, storage and \\ntransport. The labelling\
      \ and packaging is required to be easily visible and be able to \\nwithstand physical conditions and climatic\
      \ factors. \\n \\n54.6 \\nThe contractor shall approach only Authorised Recyclers of Hazardous Waste for \\\
      ndisposal of Hazardous Waste, under intimation to the Employer. \\n \\n54.7 \\nSubmittal of all environment\
      \ related documents and records pertaining to monitoring \\nand trend analysis on key parameters such as but\
      \ not limited to consumption/efficient \\nuse of resources such as energy, water, material such as cement,\
      \ fly ash, iron and \\nsteel, recycle/reuse of waste etc that shall have demonstrated continual improvement\
      \ \\nin the implementation of Environmental management System. Failure to do so the \\nemployer shall impose\
      \ appropriate penalty as indicated under penalty clause. \\n55.0 \\nEnergy Management \\n55.1  \\nThe contractor\
      \ shall use and maintain equipment so as to conserve energy and shall \\nbe able to produce demonstrable evidence\
      \ of the same upon Employer’s request. \\n \\n55.2  \\nMeasures to conserve energy include but not limited\
      \ to the following: \\ni) \\nUse of energy efficient motors and pumps  \\nii) \\nUse of energy efficient lighting,\
      \ which uses energy efficient luminaries \\niii) \\nAdequate and uniform illumination level at construction\
      \ sites suitable for the task \\niv) \\nProper size and length of cables and wires to match the rating of\
      \ equipment \\nv) \\nUse of energy efficient air conditioners \\n \\n55.3  \\nThe contractor shall design\
      \ site offices maximum daylight and minimum heat gain. The \\nrooms shall be well insulated to enhance the\
      \ efficiency of air conditioners and the use of \\nsolar films on windows may be used where feasible.   \\\
      n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n\
      \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"263\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n\
      }"

- kind: parameter
  text: The contractor must develop, institute, and maintain a Waste Management Programme (WMP) approved by the
    Engineer, and remove waste in a timely manner for disposal at authorized landfill sites after obtaining approvals
    from competent authorities such as BBMP, BDA, BMRDA, and BWSSB.

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 78,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n  \
      \                                                        262\\n\\n50.4    A suggested list of trees  / shrubs\
      \ suitable for planting and landscaping is found in\\n         Employer’s Project SHE Manual.\\n\\n\\n51.0\
      \      Felling of Trees\\n\\n\\n51.1    The contractor shall identify the number and type of trees that are\
      \ require to be felled\\n         as a result of construction of works and facilities related to Bangalore\
      \ Metro Project\\n        and inform the Employer.\\n\\n\\n51.2       All trees and shrubbery, which are not\
      \ specifically require to be cleared or removed for\\n          construction purposes, shall be preserved\
      \ and shall be protected from any damage\\n           that may be caused by Contractor’s construction operations\
      \ and equipment. The\\n          contractor shall not fell, remove or dispose of any tree or forest produce\
      \ in any land\\n        handed over to him for the construction of works and facilities related to Bangalore\\\
      n         Metro except with the previous permission obtained from the Forest Department.\\n\\n\\n51.3    The\
      \ Employer shall arrange permission from the forest department for trees to be\\n           felled or transplanted.\
      \ The Employer will permit the removal of trees or shrubs only\\n           after prior approval.\\n\\n51.4\
      \     Special care shall be exercised where trees or shrubs are exposed to injuries by\\n          construction\
      \ equipment, blasting, excavating, dumping, chemical damage or other\\n          operation and the Contractor\
      \ shall adequately protect such trees by used of protective\\n           barriers or other methods approved\
      \ by the Employer. Trees shall not be used for\\n         anchorage.\\n\\n\\n52.0     Fly Ash\\n\\n\\n52.1\
      \    The Employer may require the contractor to use fly ash as a percentage substitution of\\n         cement,\
      \ in concrete for certain structures and works.\\n\\n\\n52.2      In all such uses of Fly Ash, the contractor\
      \ shall maintain a detailed record of usage of\\n          Fly Ash. The contractor shall also collect related\
      \ details and provide to the Employer.\\n\\n\\n52.3    The reporting details on consumption of Fly Ash are\
      \ found in Employer’s SHE Manual.\\n\\n\\n53.0    Waste\\n\\n\\n53.1    The contractor is required to develop,\
      \ institute and maintain a Waste Management\\n        Programme (WMP) during the construction of the project\
      \ for his works, which may\\n          include: -\\n\\n\\n                i)     Identification of disposal\
      \ sites.\\n\\n                  ii)     Identification of quantities to be excavated and disposed off.\\n\\\
      n                  iii)    Identification of split between waste and inert material\\n\\n             iv)\
      \    Identification of amounts intended to be stored temporarily on site location of\\n              such\
      \ storage.\\n\\n           v)     Identification of intended transport means and route.\\n\\n            \
      \ vi)   Obtaining permission, where required, for disposal.\\n\\n\\n53.2     Such a mechanism  is intended\
      \ to ensure that the designation of areas  for the\\n         segregation and  temporary  storage  of  reusable\
      \ and  recyclable  materials  are\\n          incorporate into the WMP. The WMP should be prepared and submitted\
      \ to the\\n         Engineer for approval.\\n\\n53.3    The Contractor shall handle waste in a manner that\
      \ ensures they are held securely\\n          without loss or leakage thus minimizing potential for pollution.\
      \ The Contractor shall\\n          maintain and clean waste storage areas regularly.\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 74 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        47.039981842041016,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        561.0993041992188,\n\

    json_path: parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 79,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n263\\\
      n          53.4    The Contractor shall remove waste in a timely manner and disposed off at landfill sites\\\
      n                       after obtaining approval of the competent authorities namely BBMP, BDA, BMRDA,\\n\
      \              BWSSB.\\n\\n\\n          53.5     Burning of wastes is prohibited. The Contractor shall not\
      \ burn debris or vegetation or\\n                    construction waste on the site but remove it in accordance\
      \ with 50.1 above.\\n\\n\\n          53.6    The Contractor shall make arrangement to dispose of metal scrap\
      \ and other saleable\\n                 waste to authorized dealer and make available to the Employer on request,\
      \ records of\\n                 such sales.\\n\\n          54.0     Hazardous Waste Management\\n\\n     \
      \     54.1         If encountered or generated as a result of Contractor’s activity, then waste classified\\\
      n                 as hazardous under the “Hazardous Wastes (Management & Handling) Rules, 1989,\\n       \
      \         amendments 2000, 2003” shall be disposed off in a manner in compliance with the\\n             \
      \     procedure given in the rules under the aforesaid act.\\n\\n\\n          54.2     Chemicals classified\
      \ as hazardous chemicals under “Manufacture, Storage and Import\\n                      of Hazardous Chemical\
      \ Rules, 1989 of Environment (Protection) Act, 1986 shall be\\n                  disposed off in a manner\
      \ in compliance with the procedure given in the rules under the\\n                    aforesaid act.\\n\\\
      n\\n          54.3    The contractor shall identify the nature and quantity of hazardous waste generated as\\\
      n                a result of his activities and shall file a ‘Request for Authorisation’ with Bangalore\\\
      n                     Pollution Control Board along with a map showing the location of storage area.\\n\\\
      n\\n          54.4     Outside the storage area, the contractor shall place a ‘display board’, which  will\\\
      n                    display quantity and nature of hazardous waste, on date. Hazardous Waste needs to\\n\
      \                be stored in a secure place\\n\\n\\n          54.5         It shall be the responsibility\
      \ of the contractor to ensure that hazardous wastes are\\n                     stored, based on the composition,\
      \ in a manner suitable for handling, storage and\\n                      transport. The labelling and packaging\
      \ is required to be easily visible and be able to\\n                   withstand physical conditions and climatic\
      \ factors.\\n\\n\\n          54.6    The contractor shall approach only Authorised Recyclers of Hazardous\
      \ Waste for\\n                    disposal of Hazardous Waste, under intimation to the Employer.\\n\\n\\n\
      \          54.7      Submittal of all environment related documents and records pertaining to monitoring\\\
      n                and trend analysis on key parameters such as but not limited to consumption/efficient\\n\
      \                 use of resources such as energy, water, material such as cement, fly ash, iron and\\n  \
      \                     steel, recycle/reuse of waste etc that shall have demonstrated continual improvement\\\
      n                        in the implementation of Environmental management System. Failure to do so the\\\
      n                  employer shall impose appropriate penalty as indicated under penalty clause.\\n\\n    \
      \      55.0     Energy Management\\n\\n          55.1    The contractor shall use and maintain equipment so\
      \ as to conserve energy and shall\\n                be able to produce demonstrable evidence of the same upon\
      \ Employer’s request.\\n\\n\\n          55.2     Measures to conserve energy include but not limited to the\
      \ following:\\n\\n                                 i)    Use of energy efficient motors and pumps\\n\\n  \
      \                                  ii)   Use of energy efficient lighting, which uses energy efficient luminaries\\\
      n\\n                                     iii)   Adequate and uniform illumination level at construction sites\
      \ suitable for the task\\n\\n                          iv)   Proper size and length of cables and wires to\
      \ match the rating of equipment\\n\\n                      v)   Use of energy efficient air conditioners\\\
      n\\n\\n          55.3   The contractor shall design site offices maximum daylight and minimum heat gain. The\\\
      n               rooms shall be well insulated to enhance the efficiency of air conditioners and the use of\\\
      n                   solar films on windows may be used where feasible.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n         Bangalore Metro\
      \ Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 75 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        50.15998077392578,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        564.21923828125,\n\
      \        24.210372924804688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \"                    \
      \                                                                                                        \
      \       Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n\
      \        26.57955551147461,\n        88.32714080810547,\n        35.93199157714844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n        809.042724609375,\n\
      \        511.7470703125,\n        830.5714111328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                              September 2026         Section Page\
      \ 75 of 114 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.55926513671875,\n        38.09945297241211,\n\
      \        518.7679443359375,\n        778.4917602539062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"53.4  \\nThe Contractor shall remove waste in a timely manner and disposed off at landfill sites \\nafter\
      \ obtaining approval of the competent authorities namely BBMP, BDA, BMRDA, \\nBWSSB. \\n \\n53.5  \\nBurning\
      \ of wastes is prohibited. The Contractor shall not burn debris or vegetation or \\nconstruction waste on\
      \ the site but remove it in accordance with 50.1 above.  \\n \\n53.6  \\nThe Contractor shall make arrangement\
      \ to dispose of metal scrap and other saleable \\nwaste to authorized dealer and make available to the Employer\
      \ on request, records of \\nsuch sales.    \\n54.0 \\nHazardous Waste Management \\n54.1  \\nIf encountered\
      \ or generated as a result of Contractor’s activity, then waste classified \\nas hazardous under the “Hazardous\
      \ Wastes (Management & Handling) Rules, 1989, \\namendments 2000, 2003” shall be disposed off in a manner\
      \ in compliance with the \\nprocedure given in the rules under the aforesaid act.   \\n \\n54.2 \\nChemicals\
      \ classified as hazardous chemicals under “Manufacture, Storage and Import \\nof Hazardous Chemical Rules,\
      \ 1989 of Environment (Protection) Act, 1986 shall be \\ndisposed off in a manner in compliance with the procedure\
      \ given in the rules under the \\naforesaid act.   \\n \\n54.3 \\nThe contractor shall identify the nature\
      \ and quantity of hazardous waste generated as \\na result of his activities and shall file a ‘Request for\
      \ Authorisation’ with Bangalore  \\nPollution Control Board along with a map showing the location of storage\
      \ area.  \\n \\n54.4 \\nOutside the storage area, the contractor shall place a ‘display board’, which will\
      \ \\ndisplay quantity and nature of hazardous waste, on date. Hazardous Waste needs to \\nbe stored in a secure\
      \ place \\n \\n54.5 \\nIt shall be the responsibility of the contractor to ensure that hazardous wastes are\
      \ \\nstored, based on the composition, in a manner suitable for handling, storage and \\ntransport. The labelling\
      \ and packaging is required to be easily visible and be able to \\nwithstand physical conditions and climatic\
      \ factors. \\n \\n54.6 \\nThe contractor shall approach only Authorised Recyclers of Hazardous Waste for \\\
      ndisposal of Hazardous Waste, under intimation to the Employer. \\n \\n54.7 \\nSubmittal of all environment\
      \ related documents and records pertaining to monitoring \\nand trend analysis on key parameters such as but\
      \ not limited to consumption/efficient \\nuse of resources such as energy, water, material such as cement,\
      \ fly ash, iron and \\nsteel, recycle/reuse of waste etc that shall have demonstrated continual improvement\
      \ \\nin the implementation of Environmental management System. Failure to do so the \\nemployer shall impose\
      \ appropriate penalty as indicated under penalty clause. \\n55.0 \\nEnergy Management \\n55.1  \\nThe contractor\
      \ shall use and maintain equipment so as to conserve energy and shall \\nbe able to produce demonstrable evidence\
      \ of the same upon Employer’s request. \\n \\n55.2  \\nMeasures to conserve energy include but not limited\
      \ to the following: \\ni) \\nUse of energy efficient motors and pumps  \\nii) \\nUse of energy efficient lighting,\
      \ which uses energy efficient luminaries \\niii) \\nAdequate and uniform illumination level at construction\
      \ sites suitable for the task \\niv) \\nProper size and length of cables and wires to match the rating of\
      \ equipment \\nv) \\nUse of energy efficient air conditioners \\n \\n55.3  \\nThe contractor shall design\
      \ site offices maximum daylight and minimum heat gain. The \\nrooms shall be well insulated to enhance the\
      \ efficiency of air conditioners and the use of \\nsolar films on windows may be used where feasible.   \\\
      n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n\
      \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"263\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n\
      }"

      n                     Pollution Control Board along with a map showing the location of storage area.\\n\\\
      n\\n          54.4     Outside the storage area, the contractor shall place a ‘display board’, which  will\\\
      n                    display quantity and nature of hazardous waste, on date. Hazardous Waste needs to\\n\
      \                be stored in a secure place\\n\\n\\n          54.5         It shall be the responsibility\
      \ of the contractor to ensure that hazardous wastes are\\n                     stored, based on the composition,\
      \ in a manner suitable for handling, storage and\\n                      transport. The labelling and packaging\
      \ is required to be easily visible and be able to\\n                   withstand physical conditions and climatic\
      \ factors.\\n\\n\\n          54.6    The contractor shall approach only Authorised Recyclers of Hazardous\
      \ Waste for\\n                    disposal of Hazardous Waste, under intimation to the Employer.\\n\\n\\n\
      \          54.7      Submittal of all environment related documents and records pertaining to monitoring\\\
      n                and trend analysis on key parameters such as but not limited to consumption/efficient\\n\
      \                 use of resources such as energy, water, material such as cement, fly ash, iron and\\n  \
      \                     steel, recycle/reuse of waste etc that shall have demonstrated continual improvement\\\
      n                        in the implementation of Environmental management System. Failure to do so the\\\
      n                  employer shall impose appropriate penalty as indicated under penalty clause.\\n\\n    \
      \      55.0     Energy Management\\n\\n          55.1    The contractor shall use and maintain equipment so\
      \ as to conserve energy and shall\\n                be able to produce demonstrable evidence of the same upon\
      \ Employer’s request.\\n\\n\\n          55.2     Measures to conserve energy include but not limited to the\
      \ following:\\n\\n                                 i)    Use of energy efficient motors and pumps\\n\\n  \
      \                                  ii)   Use of energy efficient lighting, which uses energy efficient luminaries\\\
      n\\n                                     iii)   Adequate and uniform illumination level at construction sites\
      \ suitable for the task\\n\\n                          iv)   Proper size and length of cables and wires to\
      \ match the rating of equipment\\n\\n                      v)   Use of energy efficient air conditioners\\\
      n\\n\\n          55.3   The contractor shall design site offices maximum daylight and minimum heat gain. The\\\

      n                   solar films on windows may be used where feasible.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n         Bangalore Metro\

# Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management Overview

> Draft model-derived knowledge. Citation validation establishes supplied-source provenance, not interpretation correctness.

## Summary

- Volume 3 Part IV of the Safety, Health and Environment (SHE) Manual establishes comprehensive environmental monitoring, dust control, air quality standards, and event contingency planning obligations for BMRCL Tender Package P1.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT|Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 73, 74, 75, 76; quotation `J84ce3fcaaa4987c0`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/0`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 73, 74, 75, 76, 77, 78, 79; quotation `Sfc3bc1e59fe2f0ec`.

- The contractor must submit an Air Monitoring and Control Plan (AMCP) under the site environmental plan, maintain wash pits or vehicle cleaning facilities with high-pressure water jets at site exits, and establish an Event Contingency Plan to handle environmental exceedances.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT|Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 73, 74, 75, 76; quotation `J84ce3fcaaa4987c0`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/0`.

- Safety, Health and Environment Manual Part IV (Environmental Management) sets forth detailed contractor obligations regarding water quality protection, archaeological preservation, landscape and greenery maintenance, tree felling restrictions, fly ash usage, waste management, hazardous waste handling, and energy management.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 54.0 — Hazardous Waste Management|Clause 54.0 — Hazardous Waste Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 79; quotation `J58b0ef7d929794ef`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/7`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 52.0 — Fly Ash|Clause 52.0 — Fly Ash]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 78; quotation `Jfe28b32565bc70e2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/5`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 49.0 — Archaeological and Historical Preservation|Clause 49.0 — Archaeological and Historical Preservation]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 77; quotation `Jae8918e6335e220a`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/2`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 53.0 — Waste|Clause 53.0 — Waste]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 78, 79; quotation `Jc6a14aa0a9e5cc1f`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/6`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 50.0 — Landscape and Greenery|Clause 50.0 — Landscape and Greenery]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 77, 78; quotation `J8fb9b8a53b77aca2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/3`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 51.0 — Felling of Trees|Clause 51.0 — Felling of Trees]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 78; quotation `J7cee98218eb7ab95`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/4`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 48.0 — Water Quality|Clause 48.0 — Water Quality]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 76, 77; quotation `J347d07fa7ae4694d`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/1`.

- Volume 3 Part IV of the Safety, Health and Environment (SHE) Manual establishes environmental monitoring programs, noise and air quality parameters, event contingency plans, dust control obligations, wastewater management rules, and archaeological preservation requirements for BMRCL Tender Package P1.

## Key Parameters and Source Statements

- The contractor's environmental monitoring program requires noise monitoring at 4 locations once a week during active noise-generating operations and air quality monitoring (RSPM, SPM, CO) at 2 locations every fifteen days for two continuous 24-hour periods.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT|Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 73, 74, 75, 76; quotation `J84ce3fcaaa4987c0`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/0`.

- Suspended Particulate Matter (SPM) levels must be measured using the standard high volume sampling method set out in BIS: 5182-1981, and sound level meters must conform to IS: 9779-1981.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT|Clause 47.0 — ENVIORNMENTAL MANAGEMENT]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 73, 74, 75, 76; quotation `J84ce3fcaaa4987c0`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/0`.

- Under Clause 48.3, the contractor is prohibited from discharging dewatering or site water without Employer approval and must comply with Local Ground Water Board requirements, ensuring unused groundwater is either reused for construction or recharged directly to aquifers through approved recharge proposals while monitoring drawdown levels.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 48.0 — Water Quality|Clause 48.0 — Water Quality]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 76, 77; quotation `J347d07fa7ae4694d`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/1`.

- Clause 49.0 requires the contractor to accommodate archaeological and historical preservation concerns, consulting the Archaeological Survey of India (ASI) and other parties upon the Employer's advice to mitigate adverse construction effects on monuments.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 49.0 — Archaeological and Historical Preservation|Clause 49.0 — Archaeological and Historical Preservation]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 77; quotation `Jae8918e6335e220a`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/2`.

- Under Clause 50.0 and 51.0, the contractor must maintain ecological balance, prevent avoidable scarring of natural surroundings, and avoid felling, removing, or disposing of trees or forest produce without prior permission obtained from the Forest Department through the Employer.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 50.0 — Landscape and Greenery|Clause 50.0 — Landscape and Greenery]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 77, 78; quotation `J8fb9b8a53b77aca2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/3`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 51.0 — Felling of Trees|Clause 51.0 — Felling of Trees]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 78; quotation `J7cee98218eb7ab95`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/4`.

- Clause 52.0 and 53.0 govern fly ash and waste management, requiring the contractor to maintain detailed usage records when required by the Employer, institute a Waste Management Programme (WMP) approved by the Engineer, prohibit waste burning, and dispose of waste and metal scrap through authorized landfill sites (BBMP, BDA, BMRDA, BWSSB) and dealers.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 52.0 — Fly Ash|Clause 52.0 — Fly Ash]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 78; quotation `Jfe28b32565bc70e2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/5`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 53.0 — Waste|Clause 53.0 — Waste]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 78, 79; quotation `Jc6a14aa0a9e5cc1f`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/6`.

- Under Clause 54.0 and 55.0, hazardous wastes and chemicals must be handled in compliance with statutory rules (Hazardous Wastes Management & Handling Rules, 1989/2000/2003 and Manufacture, Storage and Import of Hazardous Chemical Rules, 1989), requiring authorization requests filed with the Bangalore Pollution Control Board, display boards, and authorized recyclers, alongside energy management measures to conserve energy and provide demonstrable evidence upon request.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 54.0 — Hazardous Waste Management|Clause 54.0 — Hazardous Waste Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 79; quotation `J58b0ef7d929794ef`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/7`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 55.0 — Energy Management|Clause 55.0 — Energy Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 79; quotation `J83d329b60a4b1d41`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/clauses/8`.

- The Contractor's monitoring program requires noise monitoring at 4 locations once a week during civil construction (Day Time Lmax, Leq, L10, L90, L50; Night Time Lmax, Leq, L10, L90, L50, Ldn) and air monitoring at 2 locations every fifteen days (RSPM, SPM, 24-hours, and CO 12 hours from 8.00 to 20.00 hrs).
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 73; quotation `Jb72f4cbacb35c83f`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/source_page_context/0`.

- Suspended Particulate Matter (SPM) levels must be measured using the standard high volume sampling method set out in BIS: 5182-1981, and sound level meters must meet IS: 9779-1981 specifications.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 73; quotation `Jb72f4cbacb35c83f`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/source_page_context/0`.

- Impact monitoring at air monitoring stations must be carried out for two continuous twenty-four-hour days every fifteen days and where perceived air quality problems exist.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 75; quotation `Jbf778e79ee5d7dc3`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/source_page_context/2`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 76; quotation `J493f5af431b2b948`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/source_page_context/3`.

- The contractor must obtain prior permission from the Forest Department via the Employer before felling, removing, or disposing of any tree or forest produce in land handed over for Bangalore Metro construction works.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 78; quotation `Jce292466578a84a2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/source_page_context/5`.

- The contractor must develop, institute, and maintain a Waste Management Programme (WMP) approved by the Engineer, and remove waste in a timely manner for disposal at authorized landfill sites after obtaining approvals from competent authorities such as BBMP, BDA, BMRDA, and BWSSB.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 78; quotation `Jce292466578a84a2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/source_page_context/5`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 79; quotation `J195a8239e4bc9422`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/source_page_context/6`.

- Burning of debris, vegetation, or construction waste on site is strictly prohibited.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 79; quotation `J195a8239e4bc9422`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_4_environmental_management_corrected/environment_clauses.json#/source_page_context/6`.

- Hazardous waste must be handled in compliance with the Hazardous Wastes (Management & Handling) Rules, 1989 and amendments, requiring a Request for Authorisation filed with the Bangalore Pollution Control Board, dedicated secure storage with display boards, and disposal through Authorized Recyclers under intimation to the Employer.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 79; quotation `J195a8239e4bc9422`. >       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "text": "Then the Engineer may require the Contractor to increase the frequency of impact\n          monitoring at any one or more of the monitoring stations until the results indicate an\n         improving and acceptable level of air quality.\n\n\n47.4.10  The Contractor shall keep records of air quality monitoring (including location, date,\n           time). The Contractor shall submit a copy of monitoring results to the Engineer. The\n           results should represent a  statistical evaluation of data by calculating maximum,\n        minimum, mean, standard deviation, geometric mean and percentile calculations for\n          evaluation of frequency distribution, trends, and comparison with emission standards.\n\n\n47.4.11  The National Ambient Air Quality Standards given in Air (Prevention and Control of\n           Pollution) Act, 1981 may be referred by the Contractor for Limit Levels of SPM in\n         ambient  air which may be followed  in estimating the  pollution  level caused by\n          Contractor’s activities.\n\n47.4.12  Where  the  Engineer  determines  that  the  recorded  dust  (TSP)  level  is\n          significantly  greater  than  the  Limit  levels,  the Engineer may  direct  the\n         Contractor to take effective remedial measures including, but not limited to,\n         reviewing dust sources and modifying working procedures.\n\n47.4.13  Where the recorded baseline levels exceed the ambient air quality standards, then at\n         such locations the action level is the recorded base line. Contractor shall take all\n           effective remedial measures to contain the levels to their baseline value as a result of\n           his activities. The action level may be varied by and at the sole discretion of the\n         Engineer.\n\n47.4.14 The Contractor shall inform the Engineer of  all steps taken to investigate cause of\n        exceedance and immediate action taken to avoid further exceedance through written\n         reports and proposals for action under an Event Contingency Plan."
>     }

>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 74,
>       "source_line_number": 72,
>       "text": "         such facility, high-pressure water jets will be directed at the wheels of vehicles to"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 74,
>       "source_line_number": 73,
>       "text": "        remove all spoil and dirt."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 74,
>       "source_line_number": 74,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 74,
>       "source_line_number": 75,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 74,
>       "source_line_number": 76,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 74,
>       "source_line_number": 77,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 3,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 4,
>       "text": "          47.3.13  The Contractor shall design and implement his blasting techniques so as to minimise"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 5,
>       "text": "                     dust, noise, vibration generation and prevention fly rock."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 6,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 7,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 8,
>       "text": "          47.3.14   Blasting technique should be consistent not only with nature and quaintly of rock to be"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 9,
>       "text": "                    blasted but also the location of blasting."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 10,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 11,
>       "text": "          47.3.15  The  contractor  shall  give  preference  to  explosives  with  better  environmental"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 12,
>       "text": "                      characteristics."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 13,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 14,
>       "text": "          47.3.16  The Contractor shall protect structures, utilities, pavements roads and other facilities"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 15,
>       "text": "                  from disfiguration and damage as a result of his activities. Where this is not possible,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 16,
>       "text": "                    the contractor  shall restore the structures,  utilities, pavements, roads and other"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 17,
>       "text": "                          facilities to their original or better, failing which the rectification/restoration work shall"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 18,
>       "text": "                be carried out at the risk and cost of the contractor."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 19,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 20,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 21,
>       "text": "          47.3.17  The Contractor shall submit to the Employer an Air Monitoring and Control Plan"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 22,
>       "text": "               (AMCP) under contract specific Site Environmental Plan to guide construction activity"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 23,
>       "text": "                     insofar as it relates to monitoring, controlling and mitigating air pollution."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 24,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 25,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 26,
>       "text": "          47.4    AIR MONITORING"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 27,
>       "text": "          47.4.1    Construction activities that will generate dust impacts include excavation (including"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 28,
>       "text": "                     related activities), material handling and stockpiling, vehicular movement, and wind"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 29,
>       "text": "                   erosion of unpaved work areas."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 30,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 31,
>       "text": "          47.4.2 The impact of fugitive dust on ambient air pollution depends on the quantity generated, as"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 32,
>       "text": "                     well as the drift potential of the dust particles injected into the atmosphere. Large dust"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 33,
>       "text": "                      particles will settle out near the source and smaller particles are likely to undergo"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 34,
>       "text": "                    dispersal over greater distance from the sources and impeded settling. SPM levels will"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 35,
>       "text": "                be monitored to evaluate the dust impact during the construction phase of the Project."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 36,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 37,
>       "text": "          47.4.3   The  Air  Quality  Monitoring and  Control Plan (AMCP)  in  contract-specific  Site"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 38,
>       "text": "                   Environmental Plan prepared by the Contractor shall establish procedures to monitor"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 39,
>       "text": "                  impact air quality and measures to control air pollution including dust suppression due"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 40,
>       "text": "                      to construction activities at work sites. This plan shall contain description of activities"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 41,
>       "text": "                      that  will cause degradation  in  air  quality, environmental procedures  to manage"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 42,
>       "text": "                     pollutants to minimise the  air  pollution, monitoring program, record keeping and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 43,
>       "text": "                      reporting."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 44,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 45,
>       "text": "          47.4.4   The Engineer shall monitor Contractor’s performance of tasks specified, and will inspect"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 46,
>       "text": "                  necessary records, reports and procedures related to the control of air quality given in"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 47,
>       "text": "               AMCP."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 48,
>       "text": "          47.4.5    Information gathered during the AMCP  will be catalogued and maintained by the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 49,
>       "text": "                    Contractor and shall be available for review by the Engineer."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 50,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 51,
>       "text": "          47.4.6   The exact location of the air monitoring stations located near air sensitive receptors"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 52,
>       "text": "                    adjoining the construction sites, such as residences, schools, hotels and hospitals and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 53,
>       "text": "                  placement of monitoring equipment thereat shall be agreed with the Engineer prior to"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 54,
>       "text": "               commencement of air monitoring program."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 55,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 56,
>       "text": "          47.4.7   Impact monitoring during the course  of the Works  shall be carried out  at the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 57,
>       "text": "                    monitoring stations for two days (continuous twenty-four hours) every fifteen days and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 58,
>       "text": "                 where there is a perceived air quality problem."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 59,
>       "text": "          47.4.8   The Contractor shall construct suitable fence, lockable gate, 220V AC power point and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 60,
>       "text": "                     suitable access at each air monitoring station.  Monitoring stations shall be free from"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 61,
>       "text": "                      local obstructions or sheltering."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 62,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 63,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 64,
>       "text": "          47.4.9   Should impact monitoring record dust levels which are:"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 65,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 66,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 67,
>       "text": "                       indicative of a deteriorating situation such that closer monitoring is reasonably"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 68,
>       "text": "                          indicated, or"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 69,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 70,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 71,
>       "text": "                      when in the opinion of the Engineer additional measurements are required in view"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 72,
>       "text": "                          of deteriorating air quality,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 73,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 74,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 75,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 75,
>       "source_line_number": 76,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 3,
>       "text": "        Then the Engineer may require the Contractor to increase the frequency of impact"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 4,
>       "text": "          monitoring at any one or more of the monitoring stations until the results indicate an"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 5,
>       "text": "         improving and acceptable level of air quality."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 6,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 7,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 8,
>       "text": "47.4.10  The Contractor shall keep records of air quality monitoring (including location, date,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 9,
>       "text": "           time). The Contractor shall submit a copy of monitoring results to the Engineer. The"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 10,
>       "text": "           results should represent a  statistical evaluation of data by calculating maximum,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 11,
>       "text": "        minimum, mean, standard deviation, geometric mean and percentile calculations for"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 12,
>       "text": "          evaluation of frequency distribution, trends, and comparison with emission standards."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 13,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 14,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 15,
>       "text": "47.4.11  The National Ambient Air Quality Standards given in Air (Prevention and Control of"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 16,
>       "text": "           Pollution) Act, 1981 may be referred by the Contractor for Limit Levels of SPM in"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 17,
>       "text": "         ambient  air which may be followed  in estimating the  pollution  level caused by"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 18,
>       "text": "          Contractor’s activities."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 19,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 20,
>       "text": "47.4.12  Where  the  Engineer  determines  that  the  recorded  dust  (TSP)  level  is"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 21,
>       "text": "          significantly  greater  than  the  Limit  levels,  the Engineer may  direct  the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 22,
>       "text": "         Contractor to take effective remedial measures including, but not limited to,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 23,
>       "text": "         reviewing dust sources and modifying working procedures."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 24,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 25,
>       "text": "47.4.13  Where the recorded baseline levels exceed the ambient air quality standards, then at"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 26,
>       "text": "         such locations the action level is the recorded base line. Contractor shall take all"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 27,
>       "text": "           effective remedial measures to contain the levels to their baseline value as a result of"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 28,
>       "text": "           his activities. The action level may be varied by and at the sole discretion of the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 29,
>       "text": "         Engineer."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 30,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 31,
>       "text": "47.4.14 The Contractor shall inform the Engineer of  all steps taken to investigate cause of"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 32,
>       "text": "        exceedance and immediate action taken to avoid further exceedance through written"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 33,
>       "text": "         reports and proposals for action under an Event Contingency Plan."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 34,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 76,
>       "source_line_number": 35,
>       "text": ""
>     },

### Sfc3bc1e59fe2f0ec

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part IV: Environmental Management]]

> This part contains Part IV (Environmental Management) of Section I (SHE - Manual) in Volume 3 of BMRCL Tender No. `BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157`.

### J58b0ef7d929794ef

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 54.0 — Hazardous Waste Management|Clause 54.0 — Hazardous Waste Management]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "54.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 79,
>       "text": "54.0     Hazardous Waste Management\n\n          54.1         If encountered or generated as a result of Contractor’s activity, then waste classified\n                 as hazardous under the “Hazardous Wastes (Management & Handling) Rules, 1989,\n                amendments 2000, 2003” shall be disposed off in a manner in compliance with the\n                  procedure given in the rules under the aforesaid act.\n\n\n          54.2     Chemicals classified as hazardous chemicals under “Manufacture, Storage and Import\n                      of Hazardous Chemical Rules, 1989 of Environment (Protection) Act, 1986 shall be\n                  disposed off in a manner in compliance with the procedure given in the rules under the\n                    aforesaid act.\n\n\n          54.3    The contractor shall identify the nature and quantity of hazardous waste generated as\n                a result of his activities and shall file a ‘Request for Authorisation’ with Bangalore\n                     Pollution Control Board along with a map showing the location of storage area.\n\n\n          54.4     Outside the storage area, the contractor shall place a ‘display board’, which  will\n                    display quantity and nature of hazardous waste, on date. Hazardous Waste needs to\n                be stored in a secure place\n\n\n          54.5         It shall be the responsibility of the contractor to ensure that hazardous wastes are\n                     stored, based on the composition, in a manner suitable for handling, storage and\n                      transport. The labelling and packaging is required to be easily visible and be able to\n                   withstand physical conditions and climatic factors.\n\n\n          54.6    The contractor shall approach only Authorised Recyclers of Hazardous Waste for\n                    disposal of Hazardous Waste, under intimation to the Employer.\n\n\n          54.7      Submittal of all environment related documents and records pertaining to monitoring\n                and trend analysis on key parameters such as but not limited to consumption/efficient\n                 use of resources such as energy, water, material such as cement, fly ash, iron and\n                       steel, recycle/reuse of waste etc that shall have demonstrated continual improvement\n                        in the implementation of Environmental management System. Failure to do so the\n                  employer shall impose appropriate penalty as indicated under penalty clause."
>     }

### Jfe28b32565bc70e2

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 52.0 — Fly Ash|Clause 52.0 — Fly Ash]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "52.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 78,
>       "text": "52.0     Fly Ash\n\n\n52.1    The Employer may require the contractor to use fly ash as a percentage substitution of\n         cement, in concrete for certain structures and works.\n\n\n52.2      In all such uses of Fly Ash, the contractor shall maintain a detailed record of usage of\n          Fly Ash. The contractor shall also collect related details and provide to the Employer.\n\n\n52.3    The reporting details on consumption of Fly Ash are found in Employer’s SHE Manual."
>     }

### Jae8918e6335e220a

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 49.0 — Archaeological and Historical Preservation|Clause 49.0 — Archaeological and Historical Preservation]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "49.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 77,
>       "text": "49.0     Archaeological and Historical Preservation\n\n          49.1    The contractor shall seek to accommodate archaeological and historical preservation\n                  concerns that may arise due to the construction of the project especially in close\n                        vicinity of such areas where such monuments may be located.\n\n\n          49.2    The contractor shall consult the Archaeological Survey of India (ASI) and other\n                      parties, on the advise of the Employer, to identify and assess construction effects and\n                 seek ways to avoid, minimize or mitigate adverse effects on such monuments.\n\n\n          49.3     Adverse  effects may  include  reasonably  foreseeable  effects  caused  by  the\n                    construction that may occur later in time, be farther removed in distance or those that\n                         alter, howsoever temporarily, the significance of the structure."
>     }
>   ],

### Jc6a14aa0a9e5cc1f

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part4#Clause 53.0 — Waste|Clause 53.0 — Waste]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "53.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 78,
>       "text": "53.0    Waste\n\n\n53.1    The contractor is required to develop, institute and maintain a Waste Management\n        Programme (WMP) during the construction of the project for his works, which may\n          include: -\n\n\n                i)     Identification of disposal sites.\n\n                  ii)     Identification of quantities to be excavated and disposed off.\n\n                  iii)    Identification of split between waste and inert material\n\n             iv)    Identification of amounts intended to be stored temporarily on site location of\n              such storage.\n\n           v)     Identification of intended transport means and route.\n\n             vi)   Obtaining permission, where required, for disposal.\n\n\n53.2     Such a mechanism  is intended to ensure that the designation of areas  for the\n         segregation and  temporary  storage  of  reusable and  recyclable  materials  are\n          incorporate into the WMP. The WMP should be prepared and submitted to the\n         Engineer for approval.\n\n53.3    The Contractor shall handle waste in a manner that ensures they are held securely\n          without loss or leakage thus minimizing potential for pollution. The Contractor shall\n          maintain and clean waste storage areas regularly."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 79,
>       "text": "53.4    The Contractor shall remove waste in a timely manner and disposed off at landfill sites\n                       after obtaining approval of the competent authorities namely BBMP, BDA, BMRDA,\n              BWSSB.\n\n\n          53.5     Burning of wastes is prohibited. The Contractor shall not burn debris or vegetation or\n                    construction waste on the site but remove it in accordance with 50.1 above.\n\n\n          53.6    The Contractor shall make arrangement to dispose of metal scrap and other saleable\n                 waste to authorized dealer and make available to the Employer on request, records of\n                 such sales."
>     }
>   ],
